Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The hermitian variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 947 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 581 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 947 + O(1/n^581). Summing over all modes and applying the hermitian convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
